金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 30 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國企業投資動能維持韌性 ：8 月標普全球美國綜合 PMI 升至 58.4，創2021 年 7 月來高點 ，
製造業、服務業同步擴張至 57 和 58.7； 8 月扣除飛機與國防的核心資本財訂單月增 1.6%，
高於前月修正後的 0.6%。其中，初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均呈現成長，顯示企業資本
支出、AI 投資與國防需求仍具韌性，持續支撐美國經濟成長。
◼ 美中同意貿易休兵再延長兩個月：美中同意調降雙邊總值 600 億美元商品的關稅，涵蓋美國

玉米、化妝品及中國家電、玩具等產品，並延長貿易休兵至明年 1 月 10 日，同時中國承諾繼
續供應稀土並擴大採購美國農產品與煤礦，美國則繼續暫停實施 50%穿透性規則；AI 方面，
美中雙方雖未就 AI 監管議題達成共識 ， 但同意建立風險溝通機制 ， 並於11 月底前展開對話 。
◼ Meta 旗下 AI 代理人 Muse 登頂排行榜：Meta 新款個人 AI 代理 Muse 上線後迅速累積逾
250 萬次下載，並登上美國 App Store 下載排行榜首位。相較過去生成式 AI 以問答與內容生
成為主，Muse 可進一步協助安排日程、搜尋資訊、處理文件及完成交易，代表 AI 正從提升
效率工具逐步走向數位勞動力，市場評價焦點也將逐漸由 AI 基礎設施擴散至應用軟體業者。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，科技供應鏈成長動能延續：AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器
DRAM、 先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持成長動能 ， 市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分
化。若 AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單

能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整體而言，科技產業獲利表現仍具關注度，惟後
續仍須留意全球景氣、產業供需變化、政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
◼ 債券佈局可關注中天期美國公債及投等債：中天期美國公債及投資級債仍值得持續關注：**雖
然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期 ， 但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應 ，
以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，
中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公
司債指數利差約 80 個基點，雖然利差偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準。
因此，在目前市場環境下，中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別，惟
後續表現仍須留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期 ，Fed 再升息風險重新升高 ：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期 ，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本 ：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」 ，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」 。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出 ，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的 「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」 ，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展 ， 引發市場對未來DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮 ， 記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵 ， 仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期 。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺 ， 留意10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者 ， 受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡 ：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

2026年金融街论坛学习体会

货币政策传导效率进一步提升   
主要政策利率即将换锚 。贷款利率定价基准趋向多元化。进一步出台稳市举
措。支持 AI 发展，推进 AI 应用。 
 10 月 19 日，2026 金融街论坛年会正式开幕，年会主题为“开放・合作・
包容——共筑全球金融发展新生态”。 
 主要政策利率 即将换锚。中国人民银行行长潘功胜发表题为《 关于当前
市场关注若干问题的思考 》的主题演讲。潘行长 在演讲中指出，DR001
“作为货币市场交易活跃、最具基准性的利率，能够灵敏反映短期资金
供求状况”，已成为央行货币政策调控的目标利率。 
考虑到当前央行公开市场操作的主要品种及利率期限均仍为 7 天，潘行
长指出，央行“将研究择机把央行公开市场主要操作品种的期限切换为
隔夜，公开市场隔夜回购利率将相应成为主要政策利率 ”。此次调整将
主要政策利率与目标利率 的期限对齐（均为隔夜） ，将央行日常流动性
管理精准至 单日，短端利率调控机制 得到有效优化， 有助于提升利率传
导效率。 
从我国利率走廊建设的角度看，2024 年央行创设临时隔夜正/逆回购工具，
利率为 7 天逆回购利率−20bp / +50bp，作为我国利率走廊边界。2026 年 6
月央行进一步优化利率走廊，将临时隔夜正/逆回购工具利率调整为 7 天
逆回购利率−20bp / + 20bp，但利率走廊操作基准利率仍为 7 天逆回购利
率。央行此次将公开市场主要操作品种期限切换 至隔夜，完成了基准利
率、走廊边界及目标利率期限 的对齐，有望进一步提升我国流动性管理
政策的精准度，平缓未来利率波动。 
 贷款利率定价基准 趋向多元化。潘行长在讲话中指出， 未来“财务能力
强的企业”可在 DR001 和 LPR 之间自主选择浮动利率贷款定价基准，LPR
不再是贷款 利率的单一基准，一定程度上赋予了企业、银行 在信贷活动
中定价基准的选择权。这一举措或能在 疏通央行政策传导渠道的同时，
降低实体融资成本。当前我国 LPR 报价按月公布，DR001 则为日频成交
价格，后者对银行间资金成本反映更加充分；以 DR001 为贷款利率定价
基准，或能 使贷款价格与银行间流动性 水平进一步对齐 ，在银行间流动
性宽松的情况下，推动“财务能力强的企业”融资成本下降。 
 进一步出台 稳市举措。潘行长在讲话中 提出“设立应对非银机构大额赎
回的流动性支持工具 ”，旨在进一步 建立债券市场稳定机制。此外，证
监会主席吴清也在讲话中提出 “加强风险研判和政策工具储备，巩固完
善稳市机制建设 ”，“扩大中长期资金来源、渠道和入市方式 ”，“壮
大市场稳定力量”。 
 支持 AI 发展，推进 AI 应用。潘行长指出央行要做好三方面工作：“密
切跟踪研判人工智能发展的宏观影响 ”；“做好金融支持人工智能产业
发展工作”；“稳妥推进人工智能在金融领域的应用” ； 并特别提出“完
善人工智能企业融资支持体系 ”，金融对科技创新的支持强度有望进一
步提升。在推进 AI 应用方面，吴清主席也在讲话中指出将“发布实施人
工智能在证券期货业安全合规应用的意见”，并促进 AI“在风控、投研
等重点场景安全高效应用”。 
 风险提示：海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性；政策效果不
及预期。

以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达 克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

完善稳定机制，深化投融资改革——2026 金融

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

一、2026金融街论坛年会 
2026 金融街论坛年会于 10 月 19 日在北京金融街开幕，会期为 10 月 19 日至 22 日，由北
京市人民政府与中国人民银行、国家金融监督管理总局、中国证监会、新华通讯社、国家外
汇管理局共同主办，主题为“开放·合作·包容——共筑全球金融发展新生态” 。 
本届年会采用“主论坛 +平行论坛+专题大会+配套活动”架构，包含主论坛、平行论坛
及三场专题大会：金融科技大会聚焦智能经济；保险大会首次由金融监管总局主办，聚焦保
险业高质量发展，并拟发布相关研究成果；投资大会聚焦跨境投资与资本对接。年会首次将
分会场拓展至非洲，在肯尼亚内罗毕设置分会场，并安排北京、香港、伦敦三地连线。四部
门主要负责人出席开幕式并释放重要政策信号。论坛兼具政策发布、行业交流、国际合作和
投融资对接功能。 
二、会议解读 
本次论坛上，央行、金融监管总局、证监会、外汇局负责人先后发言，政策信号主要体
现为：完善金融市场稳定机制，同时改善资金参与资本市场的制度条件。前者重在防止流动
性冲击被放大，后者重在提高资金配置的稳定性和效率，两者有利于改善市场运行基础。 
1、央行行长潘功胜：完善流动性支持，提高利率传导效率 
央行提出设立应对非银机构大额赎回的流动性支持工具。当资管机构面临群体性流动性
压力时，产品集中赎回可能迫使机构出售债券，债券价格下跌又会压低产品净值，诱发更多
赎回，形成负反馈。新工具对此提供应对机制，旨在防止债券市场的流动性冲击扩大并向其
他市场传递。工具在特定压力情景下启用，操作须满足宏观审慎要求、提供高等级抵押品。
此外，扩大常备借贷便利（ SLF）适用范围，重在完善银行体系的临时流动性支持渠道；推
动银行间与交易所债券市场互联互通，有助于改善跨市场交易、流动性配置和价格形成。 
利率框架改革是另一条重要线索，涉及利率调控与贷款定价的“基准体系” 。具体包括三
个方面：一是研究择机将公开市场主要操作品种的期限由 7 天切换为隔夜，使公开市场隔夜
回购利率相应成为主要政策利率，提高利率调控的精准性；二是推动贷款定价基准多元化，
未来随着相关机制完善，财务能力强的企业可与金融机构协商，自主选择 DR001 或 LPR 作为

### Page 3

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

浮动利率贷款定价基准，逐步拓宽 LPR 以外的定价选择；三是推动原存贷款基准利率退出。
该利率自 2015 年 10 月以来长期未作调整，目前在存贷款定价中已不再发挥作用，下一步将
推动司法等少数领域的计息依据改用市场化利率指标。 
2、证监会主席吴清：完善稳市机制，推进投融资改革和市场治理 
吴清的讲话围绕市场稳定、包容性改革、市场生态和对外开放展开。投资端重点是健全
长周期考核机制、拓宽中长期资金入市渠道、深化公募基金改革，并宣布证监会当日将同意
首批 18 只主动 ETF 注册。同时，鼓励有条件的上市公司加大回购增持力度。这些安排分别作
用于机构考核、产品供给和股东回报，有助于提高权益配置的稳定性。 
融资端重点是落实“两创板”改革，推进发行上市、并购重组和再融资制度调整，提高
资本市场对科技企业和不同发展阶段企业的适应性。同时，支持证券公司并购重组及后续整
合，提升投资机构服务能力；推进北证 50ETF、北交所新股市值配售等安排，完善服务创新
型中小企业的市场体系。 
市场治理方面，筹建基金业运行监测中心、规范人工智能在证券期货业的应用、完善违
规减持处罚规则和先行赔付制度，分别加强风险监测、技术治理、交易约束和投资者保护。
政策着重提高监管有效性和市场规则的可预期性，为长期投资提供制度保障。 
对外开放方面，提升 QFII 投资便利度，支持境内外机构开发更多中国资产相关产品，推
进人民币股票交易柜台和 REITs 纳入沪深港通，拓宽跨境投资渠道。同时，强调防范跨境跨
市场风险传导，关注境外挂钩 A 股个股的杠杆产品等新风险点。 
3、金融监管总局局长丁向群、外汇局局长朱鹤新：推进风险治理、
机构转型和跨境便利化 
金融监管总局强调在风险治理基础上推进机构转型和资源配置调整。丁向群提出，通过
兼并重组、市场退出等方式稳妥处置高风险机构，强化早期纠正和穿透监管，减少风险积累
及其传染。同时，在保障基础服务、考虑承载能力和保持适度竞争的条件下，因地制宜推进
中小机构减量提质，推动机构形成与自身能力相匹配的业务定位。 
金融服务结构调整同样重要。银行资金要更好适应先进制造业、服务业和基础设施建设
需要，保险则要增强灾害、航运、养老健康和科技创新等领域的保障能力。支持商业航天、

### Page 4

交易咨询业务资格：证监许可【2012】31 号

生物医药、智能机器人等领域专业共保体建设，说明科技金融不仅涉及提供融资，也涉及分
担研发和产业化风险。相关业务能否形成稳定盈利，取决于金融机构的专业服务、风险定价
和资本管理能力。 
外汇局重在提高企业跨境经营便利度，完善双向资金配置条件。朱鹤新介绍，拟推出的
增量措施涉及经常项目便利化、资本项目外汇管理优化，以及外汇市场开放和证券公司结售
汇业务试点。政策直接服务于企业跨境结算、投融资和汇率风险管理，重在降低业务办理成
本，提高资金配置效率。 
这一方向同时服务“引进来”和“走出去” 。结合央行关于人民币清算、离岸市场和流动
性供给的部署，相关政策有助于改善企业跨境经营和人民币资产配置的基础条件。对 A 股的
影响主要通过经营成本、融资条件和投资便利度传导。外资是否增加权益配置，仍取决于盈
利、估值、汇率和全球资产的相对吸引力。 
3、股指启发 
本届论坛的政策意义，主要在于稳定市场预期、改善长期投资条件，并支持企业融资与
经营。稳市机制和流动性支持，有助于减少集中赎回、被动抛售和跨市场风险传染，改善投
资者持有权益资产的信心；主动 ETF、投资便利化和长周期考核，有助于丰富配置渠道、减
少短期业绩压力对投资行为的干扰，回购增持则通过实际执行形成购买需求。这些政策对股
指的支持，需逐步落实为持续配置和盈利改善。 
对股指而言，长期投资条件改善，有利于盈利稳定、股东回报较好的企业获得配置支持 。
融资改革和科技金融则为相关企业研发、产业化和盈利增长提供条件。政策效果将通过资金
配置与企业经营逐步体现 。若盈利改善扩散至更多行业和成分股，并伴随持续资金流入，指
数修复基础将更稳固。若经营兑现偏慢或风险偏好回落，市场仍可能反复。

究领域，构建套利、趋势跟踪等交易策略。

投資建議 ： AI 運算 (GPU 、 TPU、 CPU 及 ASIC) 主導整體 DRAM
需求強勁成長，全球記憶體產業景氣循環邁入長期結構性擴張。
AI 應用領域持續擴大，推升更高容量、更高頻寬及客製化記憶
體架構需求，需求已由核心之 HBM 與 LPDDR5 全面擴展至網
路、儲存及系統管理等應用，而供應商將產能部署於 HBM 與先
進製程，對常規 DRAM 造成實質產能排擠。南亞科目前簽署長
期供應協議 (LTA) 的產能約占總出貨量的 50~70% 。不過南亞科
位元出貨量已達高點，現有廠區在 4Q27 新廠量產前產能維持
滿載，出貨量維持平穩走勢，加上公司庫存處於低檔，預估南
亞科 2027 年 bit shipment 持平，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 
127.64 元。主要 DRAM 大廠將產能重心轉向 HBM 與 DDR5 等
高階產品，壓縮 DDR4 供給，使南亞科逐步成為 DDR4 的最大
供應商，議價能力持續提升，建議南亞科 Buy，目標價 635 元。

百分點。南亞科 3Q26 稅後 EPS 25.40 元，淨值 118.99 元。

2 群益投顧 
  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。  
南亞科已連續第五年入選 DJSI 世界指數，並連續第七年入選 S&P 全球永續
年鑑，持續獲得國際永續評鑑機構肯定。  
ASP 大幅增加，南亞科 3Q26 稅後 EPS 25.40 元，淨值 118.99 元：  
受惠長期合約 (LTA) 重新簽訂帶動 ASP 跳升與 AI 運算拉貨動能增溫，南亞
科 3Q26 營業收入達新台幣 1,336.49 億元， QoQ+61.90% ， YoY+611.70% 。
受長期合約重新議價影響，平均銷售單價 (ASP)QoQ>+60% ，YoY>500% ，bit 
shipment( 位元出貨量 )則因產能受限而 QoQ-4~-6% ，匯率變動則產生 1~3%
之有利影響。  
長約到期的重新簽約為近期單價上漲的主要動力 。 3Q26 平均售價 (ASP) 季增
超過 60% ，其中超過一半的漲幅來自於半年前至一年前簽訂的高涵蓋率長
約到期並重新定價。  
終端應用結構方面， AI 基礎設施 (含伺服器 )營收貢獻顯著攀升，佔比已正式
突破 20%，其餘出貨則涵蓋儲存 SSD、網通、車用工控與消費性電子等多元

幣 697 億元，增加 177 億元，增幅約 34% ，較 2025 年資本支出新台幣 134

進設備預付款。

購上。累計 1Q26 至 3Q26 資本支出已執行新台幣 276 億元。預估 2026 年折
舊費用將較 2025 年減少 10% 左右，等到新廠 2027 年量產後，折舊費用才
會再增加。在位元出貨方面，現有廠區在 4Q27 新廠量產前產能維持滿載，

shipment 約 YoY+17~19% 。  
受惠長期合約 LTA 承諾與 AI 專案長期訂單能見度，董事會亦通過 2026-
2029 年 4 年不超過 3,466 億元的資本支出上限，全力建設 5A 新廠， 5A 新
廠全期規劃月產能達 50K 片，將導入 10nm(1B-1E) 先進製程及 EUV 技術。

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
5A 新廠第一階段月產能規劃為 35.9K 片，預計於 1Q27 進行設備移入裝機，
4Q27 開始投產貢獻出貨，目標於 2028 年底前達成每月投片量 30K 片，並
於 2030 年底前達成全產能每月投片量 50K 片之長期目標。  
此外，公司已規劃於屏東科學園區建置 3D 晶圓封裝與測試廠，全面支援先
進記憶體架構封裝需求。屏東廠長期總資本支出估計高達 100 億美元，預計
於 2H28 開始進行設備移入與裝機。南亞科 (前段晶圓級 )主導前段的晶圓級
處理 (Wafer-level processing) 與晶圓級測試 (Wafer-level testing) 。由合作夥伴
福懋科負責後續的晶圓研磨、切割、打線、晶片封裝與最終測試。  
受惠技術自主研發與客製化合作深化，南亞科藉由靈活之產能配置積極應
對市場規格演進。產品布局上維持高度多樣化策略，同時供貨 DDR5 、
LPDDR5/5X 、DDR4、LPDDR4/4X 及 DDR3 系列，針對常規應用與利基型規
格動態調配投片比例，並透過 LTA 長約機制鎖定產能與合理利潤，有效抵
禦成熟規格現貨報價波動之潛在風險。  
1B/1C/1D 和 EUV 進度  
南亞科的 10nm 製程分為 A、 B、 C、 D、 E 世代，其記憶儲存細胞 (Cell) 可進
行微縮，因此可由 1A 逐步推進至 1B 再到 1C。製程轉換照計劃進行，會視
整體景氣動態調整。 1B 製程已滿載且良率高， 2026 年產出目標占比達 40%。
1C 製程預計於 1Q27 正式交付客戶進行驗證，次世代 1D、1E 製程及極紫外
光 (EUV) 微影技術導入開發進度亦完全符合內部規劃。現有廠區在既有空間
內持續優化先進節點投片轉換，以確保 DDR5 與 LPDDR5 等高階規格產出
持續攀升；  
EUV 在 1C 和 1D 製程都有導入，甚至 1E，還是要看客戶需求，目前預期
2028 年導入採用；在興建新廠時亦會同步建置 EUV 極紫外光微影設備獨立
廠房。 1C/1D/1E 及 EUV 研發也依計畫推進。  
4Q26 成長來自價格向上，預估南亞科 4Q26 稅後 EPS 28.94 元：  
DRAM 是算力的關鍵零組件，在 AI 驅動的投資下，推動記憶體供需結構性
改變，降低景氣循環因素。 HBM、LPDDR5 與 RDIMM 需求強勁。 HBM、高
容量 DDR5 及 LPDDR5 是整體 DRAM 需求成長的主要動能，高階 e-SSD 、
BMC 、 NIC 對 DDR4 、 LPDDR4 及 DDR5 需求持續。  
一般型 DRAM 方面，高階 PC 與手機對 DDR5 及 LPDDR5 需求維持穩定；
其他消費性方面，高價消費型產品需求暢旺，低價產品需求放緩，整體而言，
AI 需求強排擠手機、 PC、車用及消費型記憶體供給，預計記憶體供給不足
預期將持續數季以上，長期 LTA ( 數年合約 )形成供需共識。  
AI 伺服器與高效能運算拉貨動能外溢，加上手機與一般運算記憶體供給端
受限， 4Q26 DRAM 產業結構性缺貨態勢維持不變。原廠庫存普遍偏低，且
供應商均依據 LTA 長約承諾進行排產，新廠建置、設備交期與新製程導入
均需要較長前置時間，整體供給彈性極為有限，供需緊俏格局為 4Q26 營運
提供基本面支撐。 4Q26 報價已確定高於 3Q26 ，南亞科多數產品合約價持續
上揚，公司對整體價格走勢樂觀，且長約占比持續提高，短單價格仍可能因
市況波動而調整。南亞科 2H26 成長主要來自 ASP，因為庫存在低點，產能
利用率已滿載，預估南亞科 4Q26 稅後 EPS 28.94 元。預估 2026 年稅後 EPS

### Page 5

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 13 日

5 群益投顧 
76.44 元。  
產能受限，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 127.64 元：  
AI 運算 (GPU 、 TPU、 CPU 及 ASIC) 主導整體 DRAM 需求強勁成長，全球記
憶體產業景氣循環邁入長期結構性擴張。 AI 應用領域持續擴大，推升更高
容量 、 更高頻寬及客製化記憶體架構需求 ， 需求已由核心之HBM 與 LPDDR5
全面擴展至網路、儲存及系統管理等應用，而供應商將產能部署於 HBM 與
先進製程，對常規 DRAM 造成實質產能排擠，使 DRAM 供需缺口將長期持
續 ， 關鍵觀察指標為北美CSP 及大型語言模型開發商的 AI 投資回報率 (ROI)
是否可持續。  
傳統應用市場在供給不足與價格調漲環境下，傳統應用產品出現規格下調
(down-specification) 或延長生命週期的狀況，低階市場部分由二手或回收
DRAM 支撐。  
DDR4 走勢：  
 供給短缺：由於主要供應商減產並將產能轉移至  HBM/DDR5 ，DDR4 市
場出現供給短缺，且此狀況預計將持續數個季度。  
 市場轉移至 DDR5 ：雖然 DDR4 市場占比逐漸變小，目前單一 DDR4 產
品仍佔整體 DRAM 市場總需求 12%~14% ， 若加計 LPDDR4 則比例更高 。
南亞科全球 DRAM 市佔率約為 3%，因此相對龐大的 DDR4 需求，為公
司提供相當充沛且健康的銷售空間。  
 需求穩固：消費性電子是 DDR4 需求最穩固的領域。 8Gb 容量是目前市
場的最佳選擇點 (sweet spot) 。每個客戶的應用中，可能有完整的基礎設
施 (平台 )，包括 PCB、晶片組等等。所以，短期對客戶來說，從 DDR4 轉
移到  DDR5 相對複雜，客戶必須在基礎設施變更上花費大量的時間和金
錢，且 16Gb DDR5 價格也上漲，成本高昂，因此需求將維持較長時間。
 南亞科 16Gb DDR4 於 2H26 開始貢獻營收。南亞科具備高度彈性，可隨
客戶需求與市場效益，在 DDR4 、 LPDDR4 、 DDR5 與 AI 專案之間彈性
調配產能。   
DDR5 ：  
 AI 運算 (GPU、TPU、CPU、ASIC) 全面驅動高容量與高頻寬記憶體需求，
加上個人電腦 (PC) 領域主要採用 DDR5 ，帶動 DDR5 需求穩健成長。  
 南亞科進度：公司已開始以模組形式出貨 DDR5 ，技術進度與市場同步，
高階的 6400 Mbps 產品已準備就緒。  
 目前 DDR5 交易量約占 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，
但仍需視整體缺貨結構而定，短期內不會過度拉升產出，以避免對客戶
造成調整壓力。 DDR5 8Gb R-DIMM 主要應用於雲端與伺服器，但在既
有產能未擴增的情況下，大量出貨仍需等待新廠投產後才有空間。

Wafer-on-Wafer (WoW) 堆疊技術：

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 13 日

6 群益投顧 
 公司正積極開發  WoW 技術，以應對未來  AI/AGI 應用所需的高頻寬與
高密度需求。  
 目前相關設備已安裝，並正與多家客戶合作開發不同領域的終端產品，
但因保密協議無法透露具體應用。預估 2027 年有營收貢獻。  
 南亞科正與多位客戶合作開發客製化  DRAM ，其中也包含  HBM 的延
伸產品以及  Wafer-to-Wafer 封裝專案。由於具體進度取決於客戶端的
市場開發時程，目前尚無法給出明確的營收貢獻指引  
 台灣擁有領先晶圓代工廠 (如台積電 )，這的確有助於簡化並推動南亞科
在客製化專案 (如  HBM 及  Wafer-to-Wafer) 上的進行。  
南亞科的長期合約 (LTA) 佔總出貨量的涵蓋率約落在 50%~70% 。 合約期限從
單季到 3 年不等，其中 3 年期的超長約已佔出貨量  20% 以上。隨著 AI 與伺
服器客戶積極爭取長期產能保障，南亞科正持續與客戶推進更長天期的供
應合約，計畫逐步將過去短天期的 1 年期合約，往 3 年以上的「長期供應合
約」進行結構性轉換，以鎖定長期合作夥伴的份額。為應對記憶體產業的週
期性波動，南亞科正與客戶洽談導入包含 「價格底線與上限」 的合約機制 (如
保證採購、價格底線或預付款 )，在保障供應商合理利潤的同時，也為客戶
鎖定成本上限。這些長期合約不僅涵蓋現有的廠房產能，也涵蓋未來新廠的
整體產能規劃。一般來說，如果是季度合約，價格會在一個季度內維持固定，
雙方都會遵守約定，並在下一次談判週期到來前盡量不作調整。  
產業結構性轉變擴大需求缺口，加速多元創新  AI 解決方案，記憶體需求
維持熱絡， AI 相關需求降低景氣循環波動， AI GPU /CPU/ASIC 晶片開發持
續推升 HBM 、 LPDDR5 及 DDR5 需求大幅成長。 AI 基礎建設帶動  eSSD 、
SmartNIC 與 BMC 採用更高容量、更高效 DRAM ，以因應高速資料處理、網
路傳輸及系統管理需求。非 AI 應用產能受限，有利於高階市場發展，使終
端產品售價提升。  
南亞科預期供需緊缺的狀況預計將持續延續至 2027 年甚至 2028 年初。南
亞科目前的策略則是技術自主、長約鎖定與利潤導向的防禦性資本支出，而
非衝產量、殺價拼市占。南亞科目前簽署長期供應協議 (LTA) 的產能約占總
出貨量的 50~70% 。不過南亞科位元出貨量已達高點，現有廠區在 4Q27 新
廠量產前產能維持滿載，出貨量維持平穩走勢，加上公司庫存處於低檔，預
估南亞科 2027 年 bit shipment 持平，價格走勢維持高檔，使南亞科毛利率
水準有望優於 2026 年，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 127.64 元。

  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

Stronger revenue momentum while GM guidance came in softer 
TSMC hosted its 3Q26 analyst meeting on October 15. While we 
view the higher full-year 2026 revenue guidance and the further 
acceleration in capacity buildout as the key positives, the GM 
guidance could be the softer spot. In the meeting, management 
further raised its 2026 revenue growth outlook to grow by around 
44% YoY (from slightly above 40%; USD term), supported by 
continued strong demand especially for AI/HPC. However, both 
3Q26 GM result and 4Q26 GM guidance came in lower than our 
expectations; we believe this is to reflect a steeper N2 ramp and 
overseas fab dilution in the near term. 
AI demand still ahead of supply, with no sign of slowdown 
Key questions among investors largely surrounded demand outlook. 
Overall, we think that management’s tone on AI demand remained 
very bullish. While TSMC did not formally update its long-term AI 
accelerator revenue CAGR, management indicated the CAGR will 
probably be higher, noting demand is extremely robust with no sign 
of slowdown and still far exceeds supply, with multiple drivers 
reinforcing this view, including - 1) agentic AI driving incremental 
CPU demand, which is excluded from TSMC’s AI accelerator 
definition and implies upside beyond the headline CAGR; 2) broader 
adoption of open-source models lowering the barrier to AI usage 
and lifting leading-edge silicon demand; 3) continued demand for 
networking chips alongside AI accelerators; and 4) TSMC’s own 
checks on US data center build progress and power supply, where 
management sees CSPs as very well prepared.  
Capacity build-out accelerating again: N2 capacity more than 
double in 2027; additional N3 fab addition; Texas under evaluation 
On N2, management for the first time guided its N2 capacity will be

### Page 3

more than double in 2027. As for N3, management announced an additional 3nm fab
expansion in Tainan on strong customer demand, with volume production in 1H28. This
comes on top of 1) the ongoing N3 fab expansion in Tainan (ramp in 1H27), 2) Arizona
fab 2 (ramp in 3Q27, slightly ahead of its prior schedule), 3) Japan fab 2 (ramp in 2028),
and 4) continued N5-to-N3 tool conversion. Beyond Arizona, TSMC confirmed it is
evaluating Texas as a potential additional US site, and is in discussion with one customer

building Terafab on utilizing that capacity; while the business model and structure
remains under discussion, TSMC highlighted that its principle is to run the fab by itself.
GM: steeper N2 dilution near term, but “no ceiling” long term
3Q26 GM declined 1.3ppt QoQ to 66.4%, primarily on dilution from the initial N2 ramp,
and 4Q26 GM is guided down a further 40bps to 66.0% at the mid-point on continued
N2 dilution and overseas fabs. Management now expects overseas fab dilution to be

closer to 2% for full-year 2026 (vs. 2-3% previously), helped by larger scale and cost
improvement, but reiterated it will widen to 3-4% in the latter part of the five-year
period as more US fabs ramp. Longer term, management stated there is “no ceiling” for
TSMC’s profitability, though any dramatic increase will not happen overnight. On pricing,
management reiterated a strategic approach by segment, location and technology, with
US production priced differently from Taiwan. Overall, we slightly lower our GM to

66.6%/66.8%/67.7% in 2026-2028E (vs. 67.2%/67.5%/67.8% previously).
CapEx: 2026 unchanged, multi-year step-up reiterated
2026 CapEx guidance was unchanged at US$60-64bn. While management did not
provide numeric guidance beyond 2026, but reiterated that 2026-2028 CapEx will be
significantly higher than the prior three years, and that revenue growth should continue
to outpace CapEx growth. Management acknowledged the tools, material and labor
inflation, but said the majority of CapEx is driven by long-term demand. Net net, we

maintain our 2026E/2027E/2028E CapEx at US$64bn/US$85bn/US$98bn.
High confidence in its structural lead
Addressing questions regarding rising competition, management remains confident in its
technology lead  for both front end wafer and advanced packaging technology. Despite
the later adoption timeframe (planned for in 2030) vs peers on High NA EUV, TSMC does
not see the later adoption than peers as a disadvantage. In advanced packaging, TSMC is
in volume production at 5x reticle size, which management believes no peer can match

today, with 9x and 14x in development and CoPoS for future cost reduction. In addition,
management also highlighted that it expects A14 (volume production in 2028) to be a
bigger node than N2, with customer engagement already exceeding N2’s at the same
stage.

### Page 5

3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance recap

4Q26 guidance: revenue well above expectations, GM below GSe 
For 4Q26, management guided revenue to US$52.3-54.3bn (+14% QoQ at the 
mid-point; based on FX [TWD/USD] of 31.8), GM to 65-67% and OpM to 56.5-58.5%. 
Revenue guidance came in well above GSe of +11.0% QoQ, supported by continued 
strong leading-edge demand including the steep N2 ramp. However, the GM mid-point 
of 66.0% is 1.3ppt below GSe of 67.3% and broadly in line with consensus of 66.1%, as 
continued N2 dilution and overseas fabs are only partly offset by productivity gains and 
cross-node capacity optimization. 
3Q26 results: revenue and EPS beat, GM below GSe 
3Q26 revenue of NT$1,494.2bn (US$46.72bn) was up 17.6% QoQ and 50.9% YoY, above 
the high end of guidance in USD terms and above GSe/consensus by 0.8%/2.1%. EPS of 
NT$29.43 was up 8.0% QoQ and 68.7% YoY, above GSe/consensus by 1.2%/3.7%; 
excluding the NT$2.24 one-off gain from the VIS disposal in 2Q26, EPS grew 18% QoQ. 
GM of 66.4% (-1.3ppt QoQ) landed in the upper half of guidance (65-67%) but 1.1ppt 
below GSe, while OpM of 58.7% beat the high end of guidance (56-58%) on stable OpEx 
at 7.8% of revenue. 
By technology, N2 contributed 8% of wafer revenue (vs. 3% in 2Q26) and N3 33% (vs. 
30%), taking advanced nodes (7nm and below) to 79% (vs. 77%). By platform, HPC rose 
22% QoQ to 68% of revenue (vs. 66%), with smartphone +10% QoQ at 21%. Inventory 
days fell 5 days QoQ to 82 days on higher wafer shipments.

Earnings revisions, valuation and key risks

Forecast changes

### Page 7

Reiterate Buy; 12m TP maintain at NT$3,300 
We maintain our 12m TP at NT$3,300  based on a target P/E of 22x (unchanged, 
benchmarked to 1.0stdv above 5-year avg. forward P/E) applied to our 2027E EPS. 
Accordingly, we maintain our 12 month TP for the ADR at US$660, based on an 
unchanged FX conversion of USD/TWD of 30.0 and unchanged ADR premium of 20%. 
Our 22x target P/E multiple is also benchmarked to TSMC’s trading average of 22x 
during its previous revenue upcycle where its 3-year revenue CAGR (2019-2021) was 
28%, vs. our 3-year revenue CAGR at 38% during 2025-2028E. 
Our TP implies 35% (ADR 54%) upside to the last close. As a result, we reiterate our Buy 
ratings for TSMC/ADR. We maintain our constructive view on TSMC’s long-term growth 
outlook as we believe the company’s solid technology leadership and execution better 
positions it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural growth 
opportunities, especially in AI.

### Page 9

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
TSMC is a leading global foundry company specializing in cutting-edge nodes. Its leading 
technology stance enables it to enjoy more than 60% revenue share in the global 
foundry market. We like TSMC as we believe its solid technology leadership and 
execution better position it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural 
growth opportunities, particularly in areas such as AI/5G/HPC/EV. In addition, valuation 
looks attractive in our view, with the shares trading at the mid-range of their 10-year 
trading history (P/E: 10-29x). Furthermore, we view TSMC as the key AI enabler among 
our Taiwan Semis coverage, thanks to its leadership stance in leading edge nodes and 
advanced packaging technology – CoWoS (chip on wafer on substrate). We believe TSMC 
will achieve its 25% revenue CAGR target for the next several years, driven primarily by 
increasing silicon content growth and AI/HPC demand, with LT GM to remain at 56%+. 
We are Buy rated.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
Valuation methodology: We have a 12m TP of NT$3,300, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12m TP of 
US$660, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

多、更多、再更多的 N3 產能擴張 
重要訊息 
台積電 3Q26 財報表現符合預期，管理層預估 2026 年營收將年增 44%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 符合預期，4Q26 展望強勁。3Q26 毛利率 66.4%，受 N2 量產爬坡稀
釋影響，較我們預估低 1.6 個百分點，但符合公司財測。受惠營收成長及營
業費用控管，營業利益及營益率均符合預期。台積電 4Q26 營收財測為 523
億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增 14%，較我們先前預估高出 7%，毛
利率及營益率則預期持平，主要受惠 HPC 需求強勁及 N2 量產爬坡。 
N3 持續擴產，並評估美國亞利桑那州以外的擴產機會 。隨 4Q26 展望轉
強，台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先 40%以上上調至 44%，並
維持全年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元。台積電過去鮮少將單一製程產能
擴充至超過原先規劃的目標( 約每月 15 萬片)。然而，除近期宣布的三座晶圓
廠(台南 Fab 18 、亞利桑那州二廠及熊本二廠) 外，台積電再宣布於台南新增
一座 N3 晶圓廠，預計於 1H28 量產。整體而言，我們預估 N3 產能將由
2026 年底的每月 15 萬至 16 萬片，在未來兩年進一步增加 8 萬片，增幅達
50%。我們認為此次擴產反映三項需求：(1)Agentic AI 帶動的 CPU 需求；
(2)HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒；以及(3)AI ASIC 。此外，台積電亦正評估在美國
亞利桑那州以外地區擴產的可能性，例如德州，但目前尚未做出最終決定。  
不會將任何商機拱手讓給競爭對手 。管理層多次強調不會讓競爭對手取得任
何商機，體現在 N3 產能的積極擴充。此外，儘管我們預期 Google(美 )委託
Intel(美 )生產的 Humufish 目前沒有備援計劃。然而，在必要情況下，台積電
將協助客戶確保產品如期交付， 暗示若 Intel 的 EMIB-T 製程進度落後，台積
電有望進一步提升市占率。部分投資人可能擔憂台積電導入 ASML High -NA
設備的時程較競爭對手晚兩年，進而影響其技術領先地位。但我們持不同看
法，採用最新設備並不必然代表技術領先。若 在 2030 年前台積電能在不投
資昂貴設備的情況下達到相近的製程效能，反而 有望取得相較同業更顯著的
成本優勢。 
投資建議  
儘管 3Q26 財報符合預期，且我們預估 4Q26 表現將更為強勁，但在市場預
期極高下，投資人仍可能對短期毛利率走勢有所疑慮。考量未來的成長動能
及穩健的執行能力，我們建議投資人趁股價回檔時逢低布局。我們僅微幅調
整獲利預估， 但隨評價基準遞延至 2028 年，我們 將目標價上調至 3,650
元，仍基於景氣上行週期平均的 20 倍本益比，維持「增加持股」評等 。 
投資風險

台灣 台積電

法說會重點數據 
 4Q26 財測： 營收預估為 523 億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增
14%；毛利率 65-67%，營益率 56.5- 58.5%。 
 2026 年展望：全年營收成長預估上調至年增 44%，維持資本支出 600
億至 640 億美元。  
 毛利率稀釋：海外擴產預計稀釋 2026 年毛利率 2 個百分點，未來幾年
稀釋幅度將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。  
 產能規劃：  
 N2 製程家族產能預計於 2027 年增加至兩倍以上。  
 台南新增 N3 晶圓廠預計於 1H27 量產，另一座 N3 晶圓廠則規劃於
1H28 量產。 
 亞利桑那州 N3 二廠已完成廠房建設，目前正進行設備搬入及安
裝，預計於 3Q27 量產，略早於原訂時程。  
 日本 N3 製程預計於 2028 年量產。 
 A14 製程已開始進行產能準備，預計於 2027 年如期試產，並於
2028 年量產。

持續有所增加。 2013 年的八吋約當產能超越一千六百萬片。

圓製造，晶圓探測，組裝和測試，光罩生產和設計服務。

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

楊志偉  
yangjway-72349@esunsec.com.tw

研 究  報 告                
樺漢(6414.TT) 
產業：電子–電腦及週邊設備
3Q26 營收季增 3%，4Q26 營收預估與 3Q26 相當 
 投資評價與建議 
未來營收成⻑端視北美建廠需求與AI ， 毛利率提升主要仰賴整併Kontron的綜
效：樺漢計畫拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔，工控物聯網成⻑將由邊緣AI帶動，
北美建廠需求使智慧工廠持續成⻑，毛利率則藉由產品組合調整、新服務模式
的滲透率與整併Kontron的綜效以向上提升。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收季增3%(年增48%) ， 智慧工廠3Q26營收季減2%(年增84%) ：3Q26
營收496.4億元季增3%(年增48%)符合預估值(496.5億元)，工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群，分別季增4%、9%及-
2%(年增44%、-9%及84%)，智慧軟體與方案事業群4Q26為政府及企業單位
結帳高峰，4Q26營收比重可望高於3Q26，有助提升毛利率。 
4Q26營收預估與3Q26相當，在手訂單達2,550億元以上： 4Q26營收預估與

產業革命的關鍵在於實體AI，將讓AI開始進入真實世界，具備『看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到』的感知、理解、決

2035年的1,200 億美元(CAGR35%)，10年預估成⻑將超過20倍；目前樺漢已全球部署超過100萬套系統，包含
Casino/Gaming (7.5萬/21萬套)、Lottery (6萬套)、ATM (25萬套)及POS/Self-Checkout (35萬套)，實體節點分布極為
廣泛；樺漢已打造⼀套共通核⼼技術平台，涵蓋Compute Platform (SOM/Edge)、Sensor Suite (Camera/LiDAR/IMU)
及Drive & Actuator、Connectivity (5G/Wi-Fi/TSN)，同⼀套平台可靈活⽀援 AMR（⾃主 移動機器人）、Mobile 
Manipulator （移動操作機器人）、Humanoid （人形機器人） 及Industrial Robot （工業機器人），大幅降低研發成本，
加速產線規模化複製落地 ； 樺漢機器人團隊已完成核⼼技術平台整合 。 早期 AMR 與工業⾃動化機器人已在智慧工廠與
物流場域大量量產；針對具備感知能力的人形機器人 （Humanoid）與 Mobile Manipulator，公司透過 NVIDIA Isaac
數位雙生進行驗證，預計於2026年起陸續展開客戶PoC與場域導入，並配合 Kontron 歐美通路擴大推廣，目標在10年
內拿下3億美元之實體AI市佔。

| 樺漢的定位與優勢 
樺漢是鴻海艦隊的工業電腦旗艦：樺漢科技（6414）是鴻海集團旗下的重要工業電腦（IPC）與工業物聯網廠，是鴻海
艦隊的工業電腦旗艦。鴻海透過旗下投資公司共持有樺漢約24%的股權，為其最大股東與⺟集團，雙方在資源、供應鏈
及高科技AI智慧製造上緊密合作。樺漢的產品應用領域非常多元，包括智慧零售、智慧監控、智慧家居、資訊安全、交
通、製造、能源、醫療、建築、園區等，且透過整合硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務積極推進ESaaS(樺漢解決方案即服
務)計畫，以滿足客戶多樣化需求。 
三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網、智慧軟體與方案及智慧工廠與廠務：樺漢的三大事業群架構分別為工業物聯網事業
群、智慧軟體與方案事業群及智慧工廠與廠務事業群；工業物聯網事業群包括邊緣運算、網安通訊、工業與嵌入式系統
整合 。 智慧軟體與方案事業群著重高毛利軟硬雲整合 ， 涵蓋工業軟體 、 機器學習 、 數位雙生 、 雲地平台系統整合 ， Kontron 
在此板塊扮演關鍵的歐洲市場與軟體加值核⼼。智慧工廠與廠務事業群提供半導體設備及廠務系統、智能系統整合、高
科技材料與設備，由帆宣(6196)主導。 
併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑，三大事業群各有所⻑：樺漢透過併購壯大規模，營收由2015年的111億元僅短短十年就快
速10倍成⻑至2025年的1,423億元 ， 尤其入股Kontron(8/20後樺漢持股49.4%)與帆宣更是關鍵 。 樺漢看中Kontron的品
牌加持與歐洲的高市佔率，目前軌道交通產品Kontron已掌握歐洲逾50%市佔，並著手把相關方案導入亞太市場。樺漢
和Kontron在技術合作方面，建立共享平台，全面整合研發、製造、供應鏈、IT和人才等資源，協同設計產品，建構具
有全球競爭力的製造系統；產品整合則共享全球品牌資源，提升市場認知度與價值定位，並深入推廣產品線至亞太、歐
洲、北美等全球市場中⼼；市場聚焦方面，結合Kontron在基礎作業系統(Kontron OS)、物聯網軟體和5G網通方面的軟

實力，以及樺漢在邊緣AI伺服器、機器人和強固型工業電腦方面的硬體優勢，共同打造實體AI技術平台。 
樺漢併購帶來營收規模快速成⻑

資料來源 : CMoney，玉山投顧整理、繪製 
實體 AI 帶來新⼀輪商機，樺漢拚五年內全球工業電腦第⼀名：實體 AI 的應用廣泛，如⾃動搬運叉車可應用於倉儲及
工廠內物料搬運，具備即時導航與避障，可提升效率並降低人力依賴。服務型機器人則可應用於清潔、導引及巡檢，
具有穩定感知與定位，可適用商辦、醫院、公共空間。樺漢認為，生成式 AI 只是開始。下⼀個十年真正改變世界的，
不只是 AI 會回答問題，而是 AI 開始進入真實世界。逐漸開始看得見、聽得懂、想得通、做得到。實體 AI 的定義應該
是讓 AI 具備感知、理解、決策與行動能力。樺漢預估 2035 年的實體 AI 市場規模可達 1,200 億美元，樺漢欲藉此商
機於五年內攻佔全球工業電腦第⼀名。樺漢目前已透過旗下全資⼦公司超恩，強化邊緣運算產品線，並結合 NVIDIA
的 Nemotron 模型，處理影像、聲音與文字等多模態資料。這類架構把資料分析留在終端端點執行，對應即時反應與
隱私要求較高的場域，也減少雲端端 AI 運算負擔。樺漢同步延伸 ESaaS(Ennoconn Solution as a Service，樺漢即服
務)服務內容，將硬體、軟體與人工智慧驅動服務整合到同⼀套方案中。樺漢 AI 聚焦在應用層與電力能源層，應用在智
慧製造、智慧城市、ESG 以及發展利基小語言模型。

利體質持續改善，智慧工廠與廠務受惠於半導體廠務需求推動高科技設備與⾃動化系統營收成⻑，工業物聯網業務則
持續由零售與金融智慧化需求推動營收增⻑。未來三年營收目標呈現年增 10％、15％、25％的階梯式成⻑。Kontron

樺漢與Kontron整併規劃

工業電腦坐⼆望⼀，寄望實體 AI 帶來的商機與併購綜效：樺漢擁有鴻海集團的⽀持，併購能力超卓，未來若能捉住 AI
實體商機爆發的時機，將帶來新的成⻑動能，毛利率的提升則視其產品組合調整、新服務模式的滲透率與整併
Kontron 的綜效而定。

| 工業電腦產業簡介 
工業電腦對環境的適應力高於個人電腦：1984年，IBM發表了5531型工業電腦，隨後於1985年推出基於Intel 80286處
理器的IBM7531，正式開啟工業電腦新紀元。工業電腦（IPC）發展至今已有40多年的歷史，從最初只負責簡單的流程
監測，進化到現今具備高效能算力的邊緣控制中⼼，其最大的特⾊在於使用「工業級用料」，能夠保障⻑達10至 15年
的使用需求，並且能克服各式嚴苛的環境挑戰，確保設備能持續穩定地運作。工業電腦最顯著的特性便是其針對惡劣環
境的耐用性，為了應對容易有大量粉塵的環境，多數的機型都採用「無風扇」和「⼀體成形」的設計，避免風扇故障引
起的熱當機或灰塵吸入造成的短路；此外，為因應戶外作業或極端氣候，大部分的工業電腦都會通過寬溫(例如：可運行
的溫度區間為 -40°C～85°C)與寬壓(例如：可輸入電壓區間為 9V～36V)測試，並搭載防塵、防水、防震、耐衝擊的機
制，確保設備在震動頻繁的環境下仍能穩定運作，工業電腦要具備嚴苛環境的適應力，高可靠度，擁有豐富的工業接口
及較⻑的生命週期管理。 
工業電腦與⼀般電腦的差異

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製

### Page 7

7 
工作溫度 、 防護等級等為評定工業電腦優劣的重要指標 ， 以冷卻機制來說 ， 個人電腦依賴主動散熱 ， 灰塵與故障風險高 ，
工業電腦則採被動、密封設計，降低⻑期失效機率；擴充與客製化方面，工業電腦可高度客製，對應工業、醫療、車載
等垂直場域需求，而非僅僅只有通用規格；電源管理能力方面，工業電腦具備抗突波與點火控制，能在不穩定供電下持
續運作；至於作業系統穩定性方面，工業電腦多採用⻑期⽀援版本，可避免更新中斷營運。 
工業電腦優劣的重要指標

資料來源 : Google、 玉山投顧整理、編製  
工業電腦的應用場域多元化：隨著技術的演進，工業電腦的應用場景早已跨越工廠的圍牆，在各個專業領域的解決方案
成功落地，從車載應用、軌道交通、船舶海事、無人載具、人型機器人、工業機器人、工業⾃動化、5G通訊網路到邊境
安防，工業電腦的應用場域多元化，隨處可見。 
工業電腦的分類與應用場景

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從工業電腦從工廠走進我們生活日常的POS、交通售票系統、醫療診斷到博弈娛樂等，它的重要性就日益加重，舉例
來說 ， 於智慧醫療其應用於影像診斷與手術輔助 ， 核⼼在於精準度與醫療級電氣安全 ， 錯誤的成本極高 ； 智慧交通方面 ，
車載與軌道監控需⻑時間運算與抗環境⼲擾，任何中斷都直接影響公共安全。

球工業電腦市場規模將由2025年的61億美元，成⻑至2035年的104億美元，年均複合成⻑率為7.9%，台廠從上游半導

AI時代帶來邊緣運算需求 
AI時代到來，工業電腦將從硬體代工走向邊緣運算平台化，軟體的整合能力將決定產品毛利率的高低，工業電腦也從第
⼀代的控制功能跨步到第⼆代的連網功能，再進而邁向大步往第三代的人工智慧及時推理功能前進。未來，工業電腦已
不再只是單純的控制設備，而是逐步成為集資料蒐集、即時運算與通訊於⼀⾝的邊緣中樞；定位於邊緣運算讓工業電腦
不再只是資料收集設備，而是真正具備「看得見、聽得懂、想得出判斷」的能力，能在工廠端即時完成視覺檢測與設備
健康判讀；更關鍵的是，這種結合正在實際解決⻑期困擾製造業的問題，那就是「經驗無法複製」，透過 AI 學習資深
工匠的判斷邏輯，工業電腦得以把隱性知識轉為可執行的數位規則，即使在技術人力短缺的情況下，生產品質依然能被
穩定維持。 
工業電腦的世代演進

資料來源 : Google，玉山投顧整理、編製 
⾃從NVIDIA執行⻑黃仁勳宣告 「每⼀家工業公司都將成為機器人公司」，並將實體AI定義為繼生成式AI之後的下⼀波科
技浪潮後，AI便不再只是雲端的虛擬智慧，而是能感知環境、理解物理規律、並在真實世界採取行動的能力，這句話所
指向的核⼼硬體，正是工業電腦。未來工業電腦由於內建NPU、GPU等AI加速模組，將可在本地端即時分析影像、震動
等高頻資料，避免等待回傳結果，具有即時推論的效能；另外，也具備低延遲響應且降低頻寬需求，再加上模型的持續
優化，讓工業電腦扮演邊緣AI推論平台，除了整合機器視覺系統的算力核⼼，同時也協作機器人與人形機器人的控制大
腦。邊緣運算正在讓工業電腦從「事後記錄者」轉變為「即時預警者」，工業電腦不再只是設備，而會成為對抗全球勞
動力短缺、突破職人經驗瓶頸的關鍵工具，推動智慧製造從「改善」走向真正的「質變」，預估全球邊緣運算的市場規
模於2033年將可突破千億美元，年均複合成⻑率將可達21%。

工業電腦(IPC)3.0於2026年後將進入主體AI時代

資料來源 : 樺漢法說會資料 
工業電腦歷經成⻑及轉型升級 ， IPC 1.0 (2011至2015年)是工業電腦時代 ， 主要定義讓設備具備運算能力 。 IPC 2.0 (2016
至2020年)是工業物聯網時代，主要是讓設備彼此連結。而IPC 3.0 (2020至2025年)為智慧平台時代，目地是讓數據產生
價值。2026年之後進入主體AI時代，將讓AI從數據分析走向實際執行，從協助決策走向⾃主行動。

Economy 
Business Checkpoint: Looking into the logistics 
15 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Small and mid-sized businesses entered Q3 in relatively solid financial condition, with profitability growth up across both 
groups, according to Bank of America business account data. Nearly half of estimated tariff payments had been refunded by 
September, though the share of these refunds relative to revenue has peaked. 
• In Bank of America business payments data, logistics spending growth per client was up four times as much for small than mid-
sized firms. Additionally, distribution and inventory transactions growth per client has outpaced spending since the spring of 
2025. 
• Growing transportation bottlenecks are re-emerging ahead of the holiday season. Trucking capacity has tightened as driver 
availability remains constrained, according to BofA Global Research. This has pushed freight rates higher and driven strong 
transportation sector payroll growth, particularly among mid-sized businesses, according to Bank of America payments data.

Business Checkpoint is a new, regular publication from Bank of America Institute. It provides a real-time view of the financial health, opportunities 
and challenges facing small and mid-sized businesses across the US economy, leveraging the depth and breadth of Bank of America's proprietary 
data. By analyzing activity among both small and mid-sized firms, Business Checkpoint offers timely insights into business profitability, hiring, 
investment and spending trends. Such data is not intended to be reflective or indicative of, and should not be relied upon as, the results of operations, 
financial condition or performance of Bank of America. 
Businesses end Q3 in fair shape 
While profitability growth remained positive for both small and mid-sized firms in the third quarter, small firms outpaced their 
mid-sized counterparts for the second consecutive quarter (Exhibit 2). Still, there’s been a notable reversal in the profitability of 
mid-sized businesses, growing this quarter after declining slightly for the past two.  
The continued rollout of tariff refunds is serving as a tailwind to some firms' cash positions. That is particularly true for small 
firms. According to Bank of America account data, by September approximately 47% of estimated International Emergency 
Economic Powers Act-related tariff payments had been refunded to companies with less than $50 million in annual revenue.

According to Bank of America business payments data, the percentage point difference in tariff refunds as a share of revenue 
for firms with less than $50 million in annual revenue spiked in June but dropped to nearly zero in September. At the same time, 
the percentage point difference in tariff payments as a share of expenses began rising again relative to the 2023 average 
(Exhibit 1). This suggests that while tariffs continue to affect companies, the refund program has provided temporary relief for 
some businesses.  
Wholesale trade along with transportation and warehousing posted solid profitability growth across business size 
However, the benefits appear uneven. Such refunds have boosted cash flow, but they have not fully insulated firms from broader 
cost pressures.  
Looking across industries, mid-sized construction and wholesale firms had the strongest profitability growth in September 
(Exhibit 3). Notably, transportation and warehousing, as well as wholesale trade were among the few sectors showing positive 
profitability growth for both small and mid-sized firms last month. 
The persistence of logistics, freight and inventory-related cost pressures may help explain why profitability gains have remained 
relatively concentrated in transportation and wholesale trade. However, these sectors also have a higher exposure to rising gas 
prices within their margins (read more on this in the August Small Business Checkpoint). 
Exhibit 3: For both small and mid-sized businesses, profitability growth was positive in transportation and warehousing as well as wholesale trade 
in September 
Profitability growth by sector and business size in September (YoY%, six-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The cost of maintaining supply chains is rising 
In fact, the prices paid component of the September services reading from the Institute for Supply Management indicates still-
building price pressure for firms (Exhibit 4). This marked the highest reading since July 2022.   
Using Bank of America internal data, we identified payments to logistics and freight companies to assess a key part of supply 
chain costs. In June, logistics and freight payments’ growth per client from small businesses grew nearly 30%, though has 
moderated to 12.7% YoY in September (Exhibit 5). Meanwhile, mid-sized firms’ payments growth in this category was up just 
3.2% YoY in September. 
The divergence suggests logistics costs may be disproportionately affecting smaller firms, who are typically less capable of 
absorbing rising costs than larger firms. Larger companies also often benefit from dedicated logistics contracts and greater 
purchasing power, while smaller ones may be more exposed to spot-market transportation costs and fluctuations in freight 
pricing.

Evidence of more moving parts in companies’ supply chains 
Yet there is increasing evidence that tariffs and geopolitics are adding complexity to small and mid-sized companies’ supply 
chains. 
In Bank of America payments data, average distribution and inventory transaction growth per business client has increased 
steadily since the spring of 2025 despite spending growth in this category falling in September (Exhibit 6). Rising transaction 
growth alongside softer spending growth suggests businesses may be engaging with more suppliers, shipments or inventory 
movements even as overall procurement outlays moderates.  
Furthermore, in May 2026, the Global Supply Chain Pressure Index reached its highest reading since the summer of 2022 and has 
continued to remain elevated into October (Exhibit 7). This further emphasizes that transportation bottlenecks, delays and 
procurement challenges are becoming more common globally. While current readings remain below the extreme levels observed 
during the pandemic, the recent move higher indicates supply chain normalization may be reversing.

per business client has increased steadily since the spring of 2025

The trials of trucking  
As of October 1, BofA Global Research's proprietary bi-weekly Truckload Demand Indicator (see Methodology) remained below 
its historical growth signal (Exhibit 9) and the Truck Capacity Indicator, which reflects shippers' views of available capacity, 
tightened significantly, falling from 39.2 to 28.4 (Exhibit 8). This suggests shippers are seeing fewer trucks available relative to 
demand than in the previous survey. 
According to BofA Global Research, the current trucking upcycle is being driven by constrained supply rather than a significant 
improvement in freight demand. While any positive movement in demand is an important indicator as the industry moves into 
peak shipping season between late September and early December, tighter capacity appears to be the dominant force 
supporting freight rates. 
Exhibit 8: Shippers’ view of available capacity has fallen 
significantly as of October 1, 2026 
BofA Truckload Capacity Indicator by year (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 9: The Demand Indicator has been sub-60 for the past two 
months after 4.5 months above 
BofA Truckload Demand Indicator (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The reduction in capacity reflects several factors, including stricter commercial driver's license (CDL) and training requirements, 
increased compliance and enforcement measures, and other regulatory changes that have reduced the pool of qualified drivers. 
This has also likely driven some wage inflation within the industry. According to Bank of America payments data, transportation 
and warehousing payroll growth per client has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses (Exhibit 10).  
Having to pay higher wages suggests labor shortages remain a challenge in the sector. Firms may increasingly need to offer 
higher compensation to attract and retain workers as trucking capacity tightens. In turn, such capacity puts upward pressure on 
freight rates, particularly in the spot market where small businesses may be more impacted. 
Exhibit 10: Transportation and warehousing payroll growth has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses

Logistics payments tend to increase in the second half of the year 
September marks a key run-up to the holiday season (read more on signs of holiday spending in the October Consumer 
Checkpoint), with spending on ocean shipping, containers, and other transportation services steadily accelerating ahead of the 
peak shopping period.  
In fact, over the past two years, the month of November has seen ocean shipping/container spending volumes well above their 
2023-2025 monthly average (Exhibit 11). The seasonal pattern likely reflects inventory restocking and holiday-related shipping 
activity. Ocean shipping volumes typically accelerate during the fall months as businesses prepare for year-end demand. 
Exhibit 11: Ocean shipping/container spending volumes were well above their 2023-2025 monthly average in November 
Logistics and freight average payments by category (monthly, indexed, 100 = monthly 2023-2025 average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Distributors and inventory payments’ growth fell ahead of holiday season 
Changes in logistics and freight spending often provide an early signal of inventory trends, as distributors account for a large 
share of supply chain-related spending (Exhibit 12). 
The sharp moderation in distribution and inventory spending growth per client for both small and mid-sized businesses indicates 
that much of the large-scale inventory accumulation seen earlier in 2025 has already occurred (Exhibit 13). In other words, 
businesses appear to be replenishing inventories for seasonal demand rather than significantly expanding stockpiles as they did 
earlier in the year. 
Overall, businesses seem to be entering the holiday season in relatively stable financial condition, supported by tariff refunds 
and positive profitability growth. However, rising logistics costs, tighter trucking capacity and elevated supply chain pressures 
suggest operating conditions are becoming more challenging.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business and/or Business Banking clients with a deposit account or a 
Small Business credit card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of 
America per Business client data represents activity spending from active Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card and at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and are 
generally defined as under $5mm in annual sales revenue. Mid-sized businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America 
and are generally defined as $5mm-$50mm in annual sales revenue. 
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Revenue tiers are determined by the combination of following factors: 1) stated revenue on small businesses credit applications, 2) actual 
account inflow into Bank of America Deposit Accounts, and 3) third party revenue estimation.  
The alternative hiring indicator consists of payments from Bank of America business clients to business-focused hiring firms which include both 
direct deposits through Automated Clearing House (ACH) and payments via credit and debit cards. 
Supply chain-related spending is comprised of payments to logistics, freight, distribution, inventory, delivery service, and trucking companies. 
These are identified from merchant names and MCC codes. 
BofA Global Research’s trucking indicators are a bi-weekly diffusion-style survey of shipper demand expectations over the next 0–3 months, 
built from responses from surveyed shippers. 
Data regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Taylor Bowley 
Economist, Bank of America Institute

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dệt may, Tiện ích khí và Bao bì & đóng gói là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị  
-2,865 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã VHM, VPB và VIC; đồng thời, tự doanh đảo chiều bán 
ròng ở mức -146 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -3,351 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 1,650 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 2,822 hợp đồng nhưng 
đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,636 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam bước vào tuần đầu tiên ở vị thế thị trường mới nổi bằng một nhịp điều 
chỉnh, khi VN-Index để mất mốc 1.800 điểm và lùi về đóng cửa quanh 1.785 điểm. Đây là 
phản ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" khá điển hình: ngay trong phiên nâng hạng chính 
thức 21/9, chỉ số mở cửa hưng phấn rồi nhanh chóng đảo chiều do áp lực chốt lời, với 
nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup (VIC, VHM, VRE) giảm mạnh dẫn dắt đà đi xuống, và sức ép lan 
rộng sang bất động sản, ngân hàng, năng lượng về cuối tuần. Điểm đáng chú ý là dòng 
vốn ngoại đã tiếp tục bán ròng ngay sau cột mốc ngày 21/9, dù đã mua ròng trở lại trong 
tuần trước đó. Bối cảnh bên ngoài cũng kém thuận lợi khi Fed vừa tăng lãi suất và phát đi 
tín hiệu diều hâu, chứng khoán Mỹ điều chỉnh, giá dầu neo cao, và lợi suất trái phiếu 
Chính phủ Mỹ tăng vọt. Trong một, hai tuần tới, bên cạnh các yếu tố ngoại biên, mùa báo 
cáo KQKD quý 3 cũng sẽ bắt đầu tác động lên diễn biến thị trường chung.

Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ tăng 
trưởng khả quan trong 2Q26 14% 
YoY, với động lực chính đến từ mảng 
dịch vụ CNTT nước ngoài

Mảng CNTT nước ngoài: Lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ IT phân cực mạnh mẽ do tác nhân 
AI (AI Agentic) s ẽ củng c ố xu hư ớng 
đầu tư vào AI

FPT ở vị thế thuận lợi cho phép 
doanh nghiệp đa dạng hóa công cụ 
AI và tối ưu chi phí vận hành

Khuyến ngh ị MUA với giá m ục tiêu 
86,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Do không còn h ạch toán FPT Telecom (FOX) là công ty con, d oanh thu 2Q2026 của 
FPT giảm mạnh còn 13,810 tỷ VND (-17% YoY). Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ không 
bị ảnh hưởng bởi thay đổi hạch toán đạt 2,568 tỷ VND (14% YoY). Mảng dịch vụ CNTT 
tại thị trường nước ngoài tiếp tục là đ ộng lực tăng trư ởng chính, đạt doanh thu và 
LNTT lần lượt là 9,868/1,538 tỷ VND (16.3% /19.3% YoY).

Sự chuyển dịch chi tiêu từ dịch vụ IT truyền thống sang chuyển đổi số và tích hợp AI 
được kỳ vọng sẽ diễn ra ngày m ột mạnh mẽ khi: i) Sự phát triển của năng l ực tính 
toán giúp kéo giảm chi phí token (đơn v ị năng lực tính toán của AI) và thúc đ ẩy nhu 
cầu sử dụng AI của doanh nghiệp; ii) Các mô hình AI mã nguồn mở gia tăng lựa chọn 
cho người dùng sẽ thúc đẩy chi tiêu

Đối với các doanh nghiệp ứng dụng sâu các mô hình AI phục vụ hoạt động kinh doanh 
như FPT , chúng tôi nhận thấy xu hướng tối ưu chi phí tốt hơn nhờ khả năng sử dụng 
các mô hình AI đ ến từ đa dạng nhà cung c ấp; với chi phí trên hi ệu năng tính toán 
được cải thi ện. Trong k ịch b ản cơ s ở, chúng tôi d ự báo biên l ợi nhuận trước thu ế 
mảng CNTT nước ngoài sẽ ổn định ở mức 15.4% nhờ duy trì chi ến lược quản trị chi 
phí hiệu quả qua đó giúp LNTT 2026/2027 đạt 6,198/7,087 tỷ VND (13.3%/14.3% YoY).

Dựa trên định giá FCFF và P/E, triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các rủi ro có 
thể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu FPT . Giá mục tiêu là 86,000 
VND/cổ phiếu.

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
58,100 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu Quý 2/2026 giảm nhẹ, đạt 2,432 tỷ VND (-3% YoY/-2% QoQ), trong khi l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ đạt 632 tỷ VND (+1% YoY/-11.5% QoQ), do doanh thu của 
mảng bất động sản suy giảm. Qua đó, REE hoàn thành lần lượt 40%/47.8% kế hoạch 
doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ 2026.

Trong Q2.2026, tổng sản lượng phát đi ện của REE tăng nh ẹ, đạt 2.8 tỷ kWh (+2.3% 
YoY) với đóng góp tăng trưởng rõ nét đến từ mảng điện gió khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 
bắt đầu vận hành; tuy nhiên  sản lượng thủy điện suy gi ảm do tác đ ộng tiêu c ực từ 
pha thời tiết El Nino, đạt 1.1 tỷ kWh.

Với năng lực chuyên môn được nâng cao, mảng M&E ghi nhận những dấu hiệu phục 
hồi tích cực trong giá trị hợp đồng ký mới trong 1H.2026, 5,865 tỷ VND (cao hơn 53.9% 
so với giá trị ký mới cả năm 2025). KBSV dự phóng doanh thu c ủa mảng M&E sẽ đạt 
4,499/5,190 tỷ VND (+28%/15% YoY) trong 2026/2027.

Hội tụ bởi 3 yếu tố: i) Định hướng phát triển kinh tế Hồ Chí Minh trở thành trung tâm 
tài chính quốc tế; ii) Hạ tầng giao thông ngày một được hoàn thiện và iii) Chi phí thuê 
cạnh tranh so với chất lượng dịch vụ của E. Town 6, KBSV dự phóng doanh thu mảng 
cho thuê văn phòng trong 2026/2027 tăng trưởng +14%/6.2% YoY.

Dựa trên đ ịnh giá SOTP , triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các r ủi ro có th ể 
phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu REE. Giá m ục tiêu là 58,100 
VND/cổ phiếu, cao hơn 28.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa tại ngày 15/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q.26 bứt phá nhờ khoản 
thu nhập bất thường của nhiệt điện 
Cà Mau và Vũng Áng

Nhiệt đi ện than duy trì công su ất ở 
mức cao nh ờ nguồn cung nhiên li ệu 
ổn định trong 2H26

Điện khí Đông Nam Bộ (NT1&2 và 
NT3&4) có triển vọng hoạt động 
thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
18,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Trong Quý 2/2026, POW ghi nhận Doanh thu và LNST cổ đông công ty mẹ đạt lần lượt 
20,308 tỷ VND (116.1% YoY) và 3,519 tỷ VND (484.6% YoY) chủ yếu nhờ các khoản thu 
nhập từ chênh lệch tiền khí của nhiệt điện Cà Mau và tỷ giá của nhiệt điện Vũng Áng. 
Với kết qu ả trên, POW hoàn thành l ần lư ợt 65%/420% kế hoạch doanh thu và l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ.

Chúng tôi cho r ằng nhà máy nhi ệt than Vũng Áng trong 2026 s ẽ được duy trì công 
suất huy động ở mức cao nhờ: i) Xác suất diễn ra El Nino tăng cao và dự kiến kéo dài 
đến đầu 2027, ii) Nguồn cung than trong nước được bảo đảm từ các đối tác (TKV, TCT 
Đông B ắc) và d ự trữ than d ồi dào c ủa doanh nghi ệp ghi nh ận ở mức cao trong 
Q2/2026.

Dự án đường dây 500kV Mạch 3 đi vào vận hành giúp giảm áp lực cạnh tranh của các 
nhà máy điện tại khu vực phía Nam, qua đó kỳ vọng doanh thu của NT1&2 trong năm 
2026 đạt 11,104 tỷ VND (5.3% YoY). Đối với dự án điện NT3&4, khoản thu nhập chênh 
lệch sản lượng theo giá công suất do sản lượng điện thực tế thấp hơn so với tỷ lệ Qc 
65% của nhà máy được duy trì sang 2H26 và 2027 sẽ giúp cho dự án ghi nhận doanh 
thu lần lượt 17,848/17,621 tỷ VND doanh thu trong 2026/2027.

Dựa trên định giá SOTP và EV/EBITDA, tri ển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các 
rủi ro có th ể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu POW. Giá m ục 
tiêu là 18,100 VND/cổ phiếu.

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trư ởng tích c ực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trư ởng khách qua c ảng hàng 
không thúc đ ẩy doanh thu t ại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ c ấu kinh doanh và hi ệu qu ả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nh ận doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 479/92 t ỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng ch ờ thương gia. Lũy k ế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 1,035/221 t ỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác tăng 
bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế gia tăng 
được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi khách, đặc biệt 
tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 182 
điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp t ừ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nh ất và 6 điểm mới tại T2 
Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Qu ốc và Gia Bình ti ếp tục mở ra 
dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, phòng 
chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh đ ược kỳ vọng tác động 
tích c ực lên biên l ợi nhu ận của AST . Chúng tôi dự phóng biên l ợi nhu ận g ộp đ ạt 
64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 48%/17% 
yoy.

Chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với AST , giá mục tiêu 76 ,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh đư ợc 
thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng hơn; 
trong khi tri ển v ọng nhóm khách 
hàng công nghi ệp b ị ảnh hư ởng b ởi 
bất lợi thuế quan

Đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyện và xử 
lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho phép 
cải thiện doanh thu và lợi nhuận của 
mảng này

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 
tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An v ận hành từ Q3/2025 giúp 
sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài c ủa các đ ợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ s ở để 
chúng tôi đi ều chỉnh tăng tăng trư ởng tiêu th ụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phư ớc trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế quan 
có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xu ất khẩu truyền thống của Việt 
Nam.

UBND TP .HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng mạnh 
từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh trong thực 
tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự án của doanh 
nghiệp ở mảng này s ẽ vận hành hi ệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và l ợi nhuận gộp 
của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ VND lần lượt trong 
2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với BWE, 
giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng 
cửa ngày 22/09/2026.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX điều chỉnh trở lại trong tuần qua sau khi gặp thử thách tại vùng cản quanh 1850, 
hình thành cây nến tuần giảm điểm nhưng với phần bóng dưới khá dài cho thấy sự tham 
gia tích cực của lực cầu bắt đáy tại vùng hỗ trợ gần. Trên khung ngày, nhịp giảm mạnh giữa 
tuần đã khiến chỉ số đánh mất đáy ngắn hạn tuần trước và chớm cắt xuống dưới SMA200, 
trong khi phiên hồi phục cuối tuần chưa cho thấy sự cải thiện rõ rệt của lực cầu. Mặc dù 
diễn biến này cho thấy áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng 
tăng ngắn hạn chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại. 
Kháng cự xa: 1880 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1830 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Earnings above all 
• Higher oil prices are in a tussle with stronger growth. We expect strong growth 
to help the global economy weather inflation risks, allowing equities to focus on the 
strength of earnings growth. Oil prices remain the key risk to monitor 
• Maintain a preference for equities over bonds and cash.  Strong earnings 
growth means we would use the recent pause to add exposure to our preferred 
markets and themes. The US and Asia ex-Japan remain our preferred regions 
• Take advantage of higher bond yields via credit risk but cap duration. The 
risk of higher bond yields looks most pressing in ultra -long maturities, but credit 
quality remains strong. We are Overweight EM USD bonds 
Hits from the 1990s 
Rising bond yields have been a key market theme.  Since mid -year, 10 -year 
government bond yields have risen approximately 40-80bps across the US, Japan and 
Euro area. Against this backdrop, gold has delivered high single -digit returns, while 
global equities have delivered low single-digit returns. 
Higher oil prices are in a tussle with robust growth data . The lack of progress 
towards a negotiated settlement in the US -Iran conflict and rising fears of shipping 
restrictions extending to the Bab al-Mandab Strait have meant that oil prices are once 
again testing the USD 100/bbl level. These worries have been exacerbated by 
increasing worries about refined product (eg. diesel, jet fuel, etc) supply. Despite these 
concerns, US growth data has held up remarkably well, as illustrated by the latest PMI 
and retail sales data. This is underpinned by AI investment growth.  
Real, rather than nominal, energy prices are key.  A chart of inflation -adjusted 
energy prices, though, illustrates why the global economy has managed to hold up 
despite these risks. Oil prices in real (inflation-adjusted) terms are closer to long term 
averages rather than at true ‘oil shock’ levels. This, together with the global economy’s 
lower oil intensity relative to history, is why we expect  growth and inflation to avoid 
worst-case scenarios as long as nominal oil prices avoid an excursion into the USD 
120-150/bbl range.

Fig. 1 Our quant model score for equities has

The Fed is likely to hike twice more by mid -2027. The 
acceleration in AI-driven investment growth means we believe 
the chances of a shift from our core soft-landing to no-landing 
economic scenario have gone up. This, combined with still -
high inflation readings mean we now expect the Fed to hike 
rates twice more by mid -2027. In contrast, the BoJ and ECB 
are expected to hike thrice and once more respectively. 
Buy-the-dip in equities 
1990s rate hikes a better parallel then 2022 . There is 
understandably some concern about whether rising central 
bank policy rates, and rising bond yields across maturities, will 
present a challenge to equity markets. However, we believe 
rate hikes in the 1990s are a better historical parallel, when 
central banks were hiking rates into strong economic and 
earnings growth, resulting in equity markets maintaining their 
pace of gains. The 2022 hiking cycle, in contrast, represented 
an inflation surprise, which we believe is not the case today. 
Strong earnings growth overwhelms other factors today. 
Still-strong economic and earnings growth means that we 
remain constructive on the outlook for equities and other risky 
assets going into year -end and beyond. The pause over the 
last couple of months was likely driven by a combination or 
rising bond yields , September seasonality and excessive 
investor optimism around mid-year.  
However, earnings expectations for global equities remain 
very strong (especially in our preferred US and Asia ex-Japan 
regional markets). We believe these earnings remain 
sufficiently robust to outweigh the drag from higher bond 
yields. Therefore, w e remain Overweight equities relative to 
bonds and cash, though we believe it is prudent to ensure the 
size of the equities Overweight is not excessive. Within Asia, 
we also trim China equities to a core holding, leaving Taiwan 
as our only regional Overwei ght, given the ongoing focus on 
AI investment-driven markets for now. 
Bumps remain part of the journey. As we laid out in our mid-
year outlook, the second half of this year is expected to be 
characterised by positive, but bumpy returns as a result of the 
factors laid out in the previous section, but also US mid -term 
election risks. The second half has thus far played out as 
expected given sufficient bumps, but still robust earnings 
growth. We would not be surprised by some volatility around 
US mid-term elections, but any further pullbacks or pauses 
would be opportunit ies to raise exposure given strong 
earnings growth is expected to ultimately dominate. 
Prefer credit over duration 
Corporate credit quality remains strong . Aggregate 
indicators of credit quality suggest credit quality remains 
strong across both Investment Grade and High Yield bonds 
across most regions. US IG bond yields face some upward 
pressure from significant hyperscaler supply, but supply -
driven spread widening tends to be relatively short -lived in 
nature and should not signal significant credit quality worries. 
Longer-maturity bonds remain at risk of further volatility. 
In contrast, we continue to be concerned that the volatility in 
very long maturity bonds could extend. Continued inflation 
and debt worries mean it is possible investors continue to 
demand a greater ‘term premium’ (ie. a premium for holding 
bonds with very  long maturities). We continue to believe 
limiting bond duration to 3-7 years remains appropriate.  
We continue to prefer  corporate and EM over government 
bonds. This implies earning a higher yield by taking on more 
credit risk (via corporate or EM bonds) rather than adding 
more duration risk. Within bonds, we remain Overweight EM 
USD bonds and view IG and HY bonds as core holdings. 
Modest USD weakness; gold to grind higher 
USD bounce led by pricing of Fed hikes . However, we 
believe Fed hikes are now excessively priced. At the same 
time, we see room for non-US rates to rise further, especially 
in Japan where the undervalued JPY is likely to strengthen. 
This is why modest USD weakness is likely to resume. 
Gold to continue grinding higher. While the move higher is 
likely to be characteri sed by two -steps-forward-one-step-
back, we believe an ultimately softer USD and continued EM 
central bank demand should result in continued gains in the 
precious metal over both the next 3- and 12-month periods.

2 Foundation: Our tactical asset allocation

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Summary

With the Federal Reserve’s latest decision to raise its benchmark rate by 25 basis points in September 2026, as well as the one 
to two additional hikes predicted  before the end of the year, we are entering a new interest -rate hiking cycle. This marks an 
important shift for investors, but higher policy rates do not necessarily imply weaker portfolio returns. What matters is why rates 
are rising, how quickly they increase, and whether economic growth can absorb tighter financial conditions. 
Our framework places the current environment in a healthy/high-yield regime. Growth remains resilient, credit spreads are 
tight and the yield curve is normalising. At the same time, the economy is approaching an important boundary. Inflation remains 
above target and the 10-year US government bond yield has reached 5%. A renewed rise in inflation or a sharper slowdown in 
activity could move markets into an unhealthy/high-yield regime. 
This argues for  measured risk-taking rather than an aggressively defensive stance . We maintain a slight overweight in 
equities, hold a strong overweight in gold and a neutral allocation to fixed income. Diversification remains key near a regim e 
boundary, and hedge funds can provide useful sources of return that are less dependent on the direction of traditional markets.

Not all interest-rate hiking cycles are the same

Traditional market 
analysis often treats 
rising rates as uniformly 
negative. History 
suggests a more 
nuanced conclusion.  
We classify hiking 
cycles along two 
dimensions: whether  
the increase in rates is 
healthy or unhealthy, 
and whether it occurs  
in a high- or low-yield 
environment. 
 Fig. 8 The different regimes of interest-rate hiking cycle 
Historical interest-rate hiking cycle defined by real growth and US 10-Year treasury yields

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; Since 1974

A healthy increase in rates is generally associated with firm 
demand, above -trend growth, resilient equities and stable 
credit markets. Central banks can move gradually and remain 
data-dependent because the economy is strong enough to 
withstand tighter fin ancial conditions. An unhealthy increase 
is more likely to be driven by supply constraints or persistent 
inflationary pressures. Growth slows, credit spreads widen, 
equities de-rate and central banks are forced to react more 
aggressively. 
The second dimension is the starting level of yields. In a high-
yield environment, bonds offer meaningful income and a 
greater cushion against moderate price declines. In a low -
yield environment, that protection is limited. This distinction 
matters because the same increase in yields can have very 
different portfolio consequences depending on the income 
available at the outset.

Reference framework between healthy and unhealthy 
tightening regimes. 
Signal Healthy hike Unhealthy hike

Inflation driver Sticky but easing Re-accelerating/ Spiking

GDP growth Resilient Slowing or  below trend

Hike pace Gradual (25bps) Aggressive (>50bps+)

FED tone Confident, data-dependent Urgent, reactive

Yield curve Steepening or flat Inverting

Equity reaction Resilient/positive De-rating/negative

Credit spreads Stable or tightening Widening

Growth against 
Inflation Positive Negative

10Y UST  High bond carry Low bond carry

Legend:    Current environment 
Source: Standard Chartered 
Assessing today’s market regime: Walking the 
fine line  
We assess the current environment as healthy/high yield 
(see Fig. 9), although it is moving closer to the boundary with 
an unhealthy/high-yield regime. Growth remains resilient, with 
2026 consensus GDP growth of around 2.1%, but it is below 
its longer-term trend. Inflation has eased , but remains above 
target, with headline CPI at 3.4% year -on-year in August 
2026, leaving the Fed focused on the balance between 
persistent price pressures and a potential growth slowdown. 
Market signals over the next 6-12 months remain broadly 
supportive. The 10-year US government bond yield is around 
5%, providing the highest bond carry since 2007, while the 
yield curve is re -steepening after its prolonged inversion.  
Equities remain positive, supported by AI -related earnings 
and soft-landing expectations, and high -yield credit spreads 
are near post-global-financial-crisis lows. 
Overall, resilient markets and benign credit conditions support 
the healthy classification. However, slower growth and above-
target inflation leave little room for disappointment. Further 
disinflation would help sustain the current regime, while sticky 
inflation, a rapid rise in yields or a sharp growth slowdown 
could push it into unhealthy territory.  
Higher rates, different outcomes 
Historical returns vary significantly across the four regimes 
(see Fig. 10), but history shows that some hiking regimes 
have generated solid returns across major asset classes. 
Unhealthy regimes are more complicated. Unhealthy/high -
yield periods (top-left corner) have historically still generated 
positive average returns, but these averages can conceal 
considerable volatility and dispersion. The most damaging 
combination has been unhealthy/low yield (as in 2022), when 
traditional 60/40 portfolios declined by an average of 15.8%. 
The lesson is that the economic cause of higher rates, and the 
level of yields, matter more than the label "rate hike" alone. 
Fig. 10 Historical returns by hiking-cycle regime  
Average returns for equities, bonds, Gold and a 60% 
equity/40% bond portfolio during rate-hiking periods. 
Hiking-cycle regime 60/40  Equity  Bonds  Gold

Healthy/High Yield 8.4% 11.3% 4.7% 5.8%

Healthy/Low Yield 4.2% 5.7% 1.8% 2.4%

Unhealthy/High Yield 8.7% 11.6% 4.3% 29.1%

Unhealthy/Low Yield -15.8% -17.7% -13.0% -0.3%

All Hike Years 6.5% 8.8% 3.6% 9.1%

Source: Bloomberg (equities and bond returns based on MSCI 
World and global aggregate benchmarks, Standard Chartered 
Why equities can coexist with 5% bond yields 
The absolute level of bond yields does not, by itself, determine 
equity performance. Investors must also consider the 
direction, speed and underlying cause of the move. 
When yields rise gradually because growth and 
investment demand are strong, corporate earnings 
growth can offset pressure on valuation multiples.  By 
contrast, a rapid increase driven by an inflation shock or 
disorderly repricing is more difficult for equities to absorb. 
Historically, several high -yield episodes combined positive 
real growth with resilient equity markets, while inflation-driven 
episodes, such as in 2022, were more challenging. 
The current level of yields has also been substantially 
incorporated into market prices. Investors have had time to 
adjust discount rates, equity valuations and financing 
assumptions to a world in which the 10-year Treasury yield is 
around 5%. This does not eliminate risk, but it means high 
yields are no longer a wholly new shock. The greater  threat 
would be a further rapid and unexpected increase in yields. 
AI investment sits at the centre of today’s market dynamic. 
Heavy spending on data centres, semiconductors, power 
generation and digital infrastructure is supporting earnings 
and helping equities remain resilient, despite elevated yields. 
At the same time, AI-related capital demand is competing with 
rising government borrowing and bond issuance. If 
productivity gains and earnings growth ultimately justify the 
investment, higher yields and stronger equities can coex ist.

### Page 9

Global Market Outlook 9

However, if bond supply outpaces available savings or AI 
returns disappoint, yields could rise more sharply and put 
pressure on valuations. The balance between productive 
investment demand and expanding sovereign debt supply 
remains a key market theme. 
Translating the hiking-cycle regimes and 
yields level into portfolio positioning 
Our positioning reflects a healthy/high -yield regime, while 
recognising we are close to the boundary with a less 
favourable environment. Historically, this regime has 
supported positive returns across equities, bonds, gold and 
60/40 portfolios. However, pe rformance has varied 
considerably depending on whether yields were rising, stable 
or falling (see Fig. 11), reinforcing the case for selective risk -
taking and broad diversification. 
Fig. 11 Asset average returns under different high-yield 
scenarios 
Historical average performance of major asset classes 
across different path of bond yields.

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered 
We maintain a slight overweight in equities, focused on US 
AI-related stocks and Asia ex -Japan. Equities performed 
particularly well when high yields were stable, historically 
returning 15.1% on average, but struggled when yields rose. 
Our moderate overweight , therefore, allows participation in 
resilient growth and earnings while limiting exposure to a 
renewed rate shock. 
We hold a strong overweight in gold, which performed well 
in both rising- and stable-yield environments and delivered the 
highest return of any asset when high yields were stable. Our 
fixed-income allocation is neutral . Although today’s yields 
offer attractive income, bonds have historically been 
vulnerable when yields were rising and underperformed when 
yield were stable. 
Diversification beyond a traditional 60/40 portfolio 
remains important  at this inflection point. Hedge funds 
generated positive returns across all regimes  - rising, stable 
and falling high -yield periods  - making them a useful 
complement when the direction of rates and the path between 
healthy and unhealthy regimes is uncertain.

### Page 10

Global Market Outlook 10

Key themes

Core scenario (soft landing, 45% probability): We have slightly reduced the probability of a soft landing from 50% by raising 
the odds of a ‘no -landing’. The global economy has  picked up steam this summer, powered by AI investments in the US and 
Asia. This is reflected in robust upgrades to corporate earnings estimates  and PMIs. Fiscal easing in Germany and Japan is 
supporting growth. Meanwhile, inflation has picked up amid higher energy prices due to a resurgen t Middle East conflict. As a 
result, the Fed turned hawkish, hiking rates for the first time since 2023. It was joined by the ECB and BoJ – the first time all 
three major central banks hiked rates in the same month. We expect the Fed to hike twice more, the ECB once more and the 
BoJ thrice more by June 2027. Nevertheless, disinflation should return by next year as energy and tariff effects fade, enabling 
the Fed and the ECB to cut rates by Q3 2027. Meanwhile, China is likely to accelerate fiscal spending to revive domestic growth.  
Upside risk (no landing, 30% probability): We raised the probability from 25% amid an acceleration in the AI-driven investment 
growth. If oil prices ease, US tax cuts, an AI -fuelled stock market boom and fiscal easing in Germany and Japan could boost 
“animal spirits” further. A global defence spending boom, led by the US and Europe, could also spur global growth. 
Downside risk (25% probability): This tail risk scenario includes 15% chance of a stagflation unfolding if geopolitical conflicts 
worsen and oil stays above USD 120/bbl. We also assign a 10% chance to a recession, potentially caused by a more rapid pace 
of rate hikes to quell inflation, a stock market downturn negating the wealth effect, or a bond selloff due to fiscal concerns.
